     1	//! Experimental Postgres backend for evaluating real Quarry execution.
     2	//! Not a deploy target: no auth host, migrations, native module, asset ledger,
     3	//! jobs, actions, or effect worker. Callers are trusted hosts, as with Engine.
     4	//! @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — evaluate storage portability before productizing it
     5	mod log;
     6	mod store;
     7	mod caps;
     8	mod contribution_upgrade;
     9	mod maintained_identity_upgrade;
    10	mod authority;
    11	mod prefetch;
    12	mod tail;
    13	mod through;
    14	#[cfg(test)]
    15	mod tests;
    16	pub use store::{SCAN_MEMORY_GUARD, WriteMode};
    17	
    18	use super::{Context, MutationOutcome, QueryOutcome, WriteState, runner::Runner, validate};
    19	use crate::{
    20	    error::{Refusal, RuntimeError},
    21	    ir::{ArgType, Program, ProgramKind},
    22	    meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter, Metered},
    23	    partition::{self, PartitionRows, SnapshotCursor, SnapshotPage, StreamPage},
    24	    schema::Schema,
    25	    value::{Value, canonical_json},
    26	};
    27	use postgres::{Client, error::SqlState};
    28	use std::collections::BTreeMap;
    29	use store::{Store, decode, namespace};
    30	use crate::rows::Rows;
    31	
    32	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, serde::Serialize)]
    33	pub struct Stats {
    34	    /// Explicit driver calls including BEGIN/COMMIT and cold prepares, not a
    35	    /// packet count. Implicit rollback when an errored transaction drops is omitted.
    36	    pub database_calls: u64,
    37	    pub transaction_retries: u64,
    38	    pub prefetch_marked_reads: u64,
    39	    /// Largest marked page in the most recent transaction.
    40	    pub prefetch_page_reads: u64,
    41	    pub prefetch_rows_transferred: u64,
    42	    pub prefetch_bytes_transferred: u64,
    43	}
    44	
    45	pub struct PostgresEngine {
    46	    store: Store,
    47	    schema: Schema,
    48	    programs: BTreeMap<String, Program>,
    49	    ceilings: Ceilings,
    50	    mode: WriteMode,
    51	    retries: u64,
    52	    precharge: Metered,
    53	    expiry_after: BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>,
    54	}
    55	
    56	impl PostgresEngine {
    57	    /// Create a NEW, isolated sb4_eval_* namespace; never replace an existing
    58	    /// schema. TLS/credentials are owned by the caller's configured Client.
    59	    pub fn create(
    60	        client: Client,
    61	        name: &str,
    62	        mut schema: Schema,
    63	        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
    64	    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
    65	        let name = namespace(name)?;
    66	        refuse_maintained_columns(&schema)?;
    67	        super::columns::validate_before_prepare(&schema)?;
    68	        schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
    69	        let programs: BTreeMap<_, _> = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
    70	        supported(&schema, &programs)?;
    71	        Self::attach(client, schema, programs, Some(&name))
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    /// Reopen the persisted compiled generation; subsequent transactions refuse
    75	    /// if another connection activates a successor.
    76	    pub fn open(mut client: Client, name: &str) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
    77	        let name = namespace(name)?;
    78	        client.batch_execute(&format!("SET search_path = {name}, pg_catalog;"))?;
    79	        let row = client.query_one("SELECT version,schema,programs FROM _backend WHERE singleton", &[])?;
    80	        if row.get::<_, &str>(0) != store::VERSION {
    81	            return Err(RuntimeError::Store("not this Postgres evaluation store version".into()));
    82	        }
    83	        let schema = decode(row.get(1))?;
    84	        let programs = decode(row.get(2))?;
    85	        supported(&schema, &programs)?;
    86	        // Existing immutable stores adopt this portable admission policy at open.
    87	        // Return one offending index and its exact count, never its row payloads.
    88	        if let Some(row) = client.query(
    89	            "SELECT t, ix, COUNT(*) FROM i WHERE octet_length(k) > $1 GROUP BY t, ix ORDER BY t, ix LIMIT 1",
    90	            &[&(crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as i32)],
    91	        )?.first() {
    92	            return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(row.get(0), row.get(1), row.get::<_, i64>(2) as u64));
    93	        }
    94	        Self::attach(client, schema, programs, None)
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — read-session storage and all other upgrades
    98	    // commit together, only after every open/adoption check has succeeded.
    99	    fn attach(client: Client, mut schema: Schema, programs: BTreeMap<String, Program>, create: Option<&str>) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
   100	        let mut store = Store::new(client);
   101	        let tx = store.opening_transaction()?;
   102	        tx.batch("SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '5s'; SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';")?;
   103	        if let Some(name) = create {
   104	            tx.batch(&format!("CREATE SCHEMA {name}; SET search_path = {name}, pg_catalog;"))?;
   105	            tx.batch(store::DDL)?;
   106	            tx.execute("INSERT INTO _backend VALUES (true,$1,$2,$3)",
   107	                &[&store::VERSION, &canonical_json(&schema), &canonical_json(&programs)])?;
   108	        }
   109	        tx.query("SELECT seq FROM _head WHERE singleton FOR UPDATE", &[])?;
   110	        // Admission must use the backend that survived the wait for the head.
   111	        // A format change can leave schema/program bytes unchanged.
   112	        let held = tx.query("SELECT version,schema,programs FROM _backend WHERE singleton", &[])?.remove(0);
   113	        if held.get::<_, &str>(0) != store::VERSION {
   114	            return Err(RuntimeError::Store("not this Postgres evaluation store version".into()));
   115	        }
   116	        let held_schema: Schema = decode(held.get(1))?;
   117	        let held_programs: BTreeMap<String, Program> = decode(held.get(2))?;
   118	        supported(&held_schema, &held_programs)?;
   119	        if held_schema.hash() != schema.hash() || canonical_json(&held_programs) != canonical_json(&programs) {
   120	            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("the backend changed while opening; reopen its current generation".into()));
   121	        }
   122	        // Even a no-op ALTER takes an exclusive table lock. Reopening an
   123	        // upgraded store must remain possible while another reader holds it.
   124	        for (column, ddl) in [
   125	            ("generation", "ALTER TABLE _backend ADD COLUMN generation BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1"),
   126	            ("through_shortened", "ALTER TABLE _backend ADD COLUMN through_shortened TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'"),
   127	            ("maintained_identity_version", "ALTER TABLE _backend ADD COLUMN maintained_identity_version TEXT"),
   128	        ] {
   129	            let exists: bool = tx.query("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid = '_backend'::regclass AND attname = $1 AND NOT attisdropped)", &[&column])?[0].get(0);
   130	            if !exists { tx.batch(ddl)?; }
   131	        }
   132	        // Repeat admission under the head fence used by physical adoption.
   133	        if let Some(row) = tx.query(
   134	            "SELECT t, ix, COUNT(*) FROM i WHERE octet_length(k) > $1 GROUP BY t, ix ORDER BY t, ix LIMIT 1",
   135	            &[&(crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as i32)],
   136	        )?.first() {
   137	            return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(row.get(0), row.get(1), row.get::<_, i64>(2) as u64));
   138	        }
   139	        tx.batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _sequences (parent TEXT NOT NULL, id TEXT NOT NULL, value BIGINT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(parent, id));")?;
   140	        tx.batch(store::READ_SESSIONS_DDL)?;
   141	        tx.batch("DROP INDEX IF EXISTS _log_row_seq; DROP INDEX IF EXISTS _log_row_seq_kind;")?;
   142	        caps::install(&tx, &schema)?;
   143	        let generation = contribution_upgrade::install(&tx, &mut schema)?;
   144	        let generation = maintained_identity_upgrade::install(&tx, &schema, create.is_some(), generation)?;
   145	        // Retain the adapter's ordinary connection limits on successful open.
   146	        tx.batch("SET statement_timeout = '5s'; SET lock_timeout = '5s';")?;
   147	        tx.commit()?;
   148	        store.generation = generation;
   149	        schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
   150	        Ok(Self { store, schema, programs, ceilings: Ceilings::default(), mode: WriteMode::Serialized, retries: 0, precharge: Metered::default(), expiry_after: BTreeMap::new() })
   151	    }
   152

   154	        &self.schema
   155	    }
   156	    pub fn programs(&self) -> &BTreeMap<String, Program> {
   157	        &self.programs
   158	    }

   170	    /// Differential evaluation: reserve budget before the first logical charge.
   171	    pub fn set_meter_precharge(&mut self, used: Metered) { self.precharge = used; }

   175	            prefetch_marked_reads: self.store.marked_reads.get(), prefetch_page_reads: self.store.page_reads.get(), prefetch_rows_transferred: self.store.prefetch_rows.get(),
   176	            prefetch_bytes_transferred: self.store.prefetch_bytes.get() }

   179	    pub fn query(&mut self, name: &str, context: Context) -> Result<QueryOutcome, Refusal> {
   180	        self.query_with_delivery(name, context, super::DeliveryManifest::Build)
   181	    }
   182	
   183	    pub fn query_with_delivery(&mut self, name: &str, mut context: Context, manifest: super::DeliveryManifest) -> Result<QueryOutcome, Refusal> {
   184	        context.generation = self.generation();
   185	        let program = self.programs.get(name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::UnknownOp(name.into()))?;
   186	        if program.kind != ProgramKind::Query {
   187	            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is not a query")).into());
   188	        }
   189	        super::server_query_allowed(&self.schema, name)?;
   190	        validate::validate_inputs(&self.schema, program, &mut context.args)?;
   191	        context.now.get_or_insert_with(crate::now_ms);
   192	        let tx = self.store.transaction(None)?;
   193	        let seq = tx.next_seq()? - 1;
   194	        let mut runner = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, context, Meter::new(self.ceilings, Cut::Cap), seq);
   195	        runner.meter.used = self.precharge;
   196	        let data = runner.run(program)?;
   197	        runner.charge_output(&data)?;
   198	        let (delivery, delivery_meter) = match manifest { super::DeliveryManifest::Build => runner.delivery_manifest(&data), super::DeliveryManifest::Skip => (None, None) };
   199	        let outcome = QueryOutcome { decoration_expiry: runner.decoration_expiry, search_rows: runner.search_rows.take(), search_scopes: None, delivery, delivery_meter, ephemeral: None,
   200	            unavailable: runner.query_unavailable,
     1	//! The same capture barrier, restore validation and child-first sweep on Postgres.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.014#7-activation — publish captures, indexes and generation in one transaction.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	impl PostgresEngine {
     6	    pub fn read_through_shortened(&mut self) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, u64>, RuntimeError> {
     7	        let tx = self.store.transaction(None)?;
     8	        let counts = decode(
     9	            tx.query(
    10	                "SELECT through_shortened FROM _backend WHERE singleton",
    11	                &[],
    12	            )?[0]
    13	                .get(0),
    14	        )?;
    15	        tx.rollback()?;
    16	        Ok(counts)
    17	    }
    18	    pub fn sweep_expired(&mut self, now: i64, limit: usize) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    19	        let tx = self.store.transaction(Some(WriteMode::Serialized))?;
    20	        let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
    21	        let mut runner = Runner::new(
    22	            &tx,
    23	            &self.schema,
    24	            Context::system().at(now),
    25	            Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort),
    26	            seq,
    27	        );
    28	        let mut expiry_after = self.expiry_after.clone();
    29	        let count = runner.sweep_server_rows(limit, &mut expiry_after)?;
    30	        let events = runner.events.take();
    31	        let tables: Vec<_> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
    32	        let writes = runner.rows_written;
    33	        drop(runner);
    34	        if writes > 0 {
    35	            tx.record_commit(
    36	                seq,
    37	                now,
    38	                "expire",
    39	                crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,
    40	                "sweep",
    41	                &tables,
    42	                writes,
    43	            )?;
    44	            partition::append(&tx, seq, &events)?;
    45	        }
    46	        tx.commit()?;
    47	        self.expiry_after = expiry_after;
    48	        Ok(count)
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    pub fn load_trusted(
    52	        &mut self,
    53	        rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>,
    54	        now: i64,
    55	    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    56	        let tx = self.store.transaction(Some(WriteMode::Serialized))?;
    57	        let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
    58	        let order = self.schema.through_order();
    59	        let mut rows: Vec<_> = rows.into_iter().collect();
    60	        rows.sort_by_key(|(n, _)| order.iter().position(|x| x == n));
    61	        let mut count = 0;
    62	        let mut runner = Runner::new(
    63	            &tx,
    64	            &self.schema,
    65	            Context::system().at(now),
    66	            Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort),
    67	            seq,
    68	        );
    69	        for (name, mut row) in rows {
    70	            let table = self
    71	                .schema
    72	                .table(&name)
    73	                .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {name}")))?;
    74	            table.recompute_deadline(&mut row)?;
    75	            validate::validate_row(&self.schema, &name, &row)?;
    76	            let old = tx.get(&name, row.field("id")?.as_str()?)?;
    77	            tx.put(&name, table, &row, old.as_ref())?;
    78	            partition::row_changed(
    79	                &mut runner,
    80	                &name,
    81	                old.as_ref(),
    82	                Some(&row),
    83	                crate::engine::write::Origin::Source,
    84	            )?;
    85	            runner.written_tables.insert(name);
    86	            count += 1;
    87	        }
    88	        super::super::through_activation::validate_restore(&tx, &self.schema, now)?;
    89	        let events = runner.events.take();
    90	        let tables: Vec<_> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
    91	        drop(runner);
    92	        if count > 0 {
    93	            tx.record_commit(
    94	                seq,
    95	                now,
    96	                "restore",
    97	                crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,
    98	                "restore",
    99	                &tables,
   100	                count,
   101	            )?;
   102	            partition::append(&tx, seq, &events)?;
   103	        }
   104	        tx.commit()?;
   105	        Ok(count)
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    pub fn adopt_at(
   109	        &mut self,
   110	        mut schema: Schema,
   111	        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
   112	        now: i64,
   113	    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   114	        schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
   115	        // @ref LLP 3000.022#5 — adoption replaces tracked membership and any shared binding index.
   116	        schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
   117	        let programs: BTreeMap<_, _> = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
   118	        supported(&schema, &programs)?;
   119	        let generation = self
   120	            .generation()
   121	            .checked_add(1)
   122	            .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Generation("generation overflow".into()))?;
   123	        let tx = self.store.transaction(Some(WriteMode::Serialized))?;
   124	        let shortened =
   125	            super::super::through_activation::activate(&tx, &schema, Some(&self.schema), now)?;
   126	        tx.execute("DELETE FROM i", &[])?;
   127	        tx.execute("DELETE FROM _cap_counts", &[])?;
   128	        for name in self
   129	            .schema
   130	            .tables
   131	            .keys()
   132	            .filter(|n| !schema.tables.contains_key(*n))
   133	        {
   134	            tx.execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=$1", &[name])?;
   135	        }
   136	        for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
   137	            let mut after = String::new();
   138	            loop {
   139	                let page = tx.query(
   140	                    "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=$1 AND id>$2 ORDER BY id LIMIT 256",
   141	                    &[name, &after],
   142	                )?;
   143	                if page.is_empty() {
   144	                    break;
   145	                }
   146	                for item in page {
   147	                    after = item.get(0);
   148	                    let mut row: Value = decode(item.get(1))?;
   149	                    if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
   150	                        fields.retain(|f, _| table.columns.contains_key(f));
   151	                    }
   152	                    table.recompute_deadline(&mut row)?;
   153	                    validate::validate_row(&schema, name, &crate::schema::row_image(&row, table))?;
   154	                    tx.execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=$1 AND id=$2", &[name, &after])?;
   155	                    tx.put(name, table, &row, None)?;
   156	                }
   157	            }
   158	        }
   159	        let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
   160	        let mut runner = Runner::new(
   161	            &tx,
   162	            &schema,
   163	            Context::system().at(now),
   164	            Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort),
   165	            seq,
   166	        );
   167	        runner.event_free = true;
   168	        for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()) {
   169	            runner.rebuild_maintain(m)?;
   170	        }
   171	        let parents = super::super::column_activation::changed_parents(&schema, Some(&self.schema));
   172	        runner.activate_columns(
   173	            Some(&self.schema),
   174	            &schema.maintains.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
   175	            &parents,
   176	            None,
   177	        )?;
   178	        drop(runner);
   179	        super::super::through_activation::validate_indexes(&tx, &schema)?;
   180	        tx.execute("UPDATE _backend SET schema=$1, programs=$2, generation=$3, through_shortened=$4, maintained_identity_version=$5 WHERE singleton",&[
   181	            &canonical_json(&schema), &canonical_json(&programs), &(generation as i64),
   182	            &canonical_json(&shortened), &crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION,
   183	        ])?;
   184	        tx.commit()?;
   185	        self.schema = schema;
   186	        self.programs = programs;
   187	        self.store.generation = generation;
   188	        self.expiry_after.clear();
   189	        Ok(generation)
   190	    }
   191	}

codex
The correction adds a table cursor that publishes after commit, and the sweep now checks cascade read-through blockers before set-null writes. I’m tracing the remaining error paths and the ungrouped identity callers to check whether either can still leave inconsistent state.

     2	use super::runner::Runner;

    21	impl Runner<'_> {
    22	    // @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — no predicted delta enters receipt-derived state.
    23	    pub(super) fn defer_receipt_maintain(&self, m: &Maintain) -> bool {
    24	        self.device && self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target)
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    // Cache ordinary group keys once per target/query. Predicates and receipt
    28	    // fields are never evaluated; unknown/computed keys conservatively freeze
    29	    // the target. An unpinned read is incomplete if any group is dirty, while
    30	    // each returned row still decides independently whether to recount.
    31	    #[inline(never)]
    32	    pub(super) fn receipt_maintain_pending(&mut self, m: &Maintain, group: Option<&[Value]>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    33	        if !self.defer_receipt_maintain(m) { return Ok(false); }
    34	        if !self.receipt_pending_groups.contains_key(&m.target) {
    35	            let fields = if let Some(k) = &m.row_key { vec![k.source_key.clone()] }
    36	                else { m.by.iter().map(|f| m.aliases.get(f).unwrap_or(f).clone()).collect() };
    37	            let mut groups = self.receipt_overlay_groups(&m.source, &fields, !m.computed.is_empty())?;
    38	            if let Some(k) = &m.row_key {
    39	                let member = self.receipt_overlay_groups(&k.table, std::slice::from_ref(&k.member_key), false)?;
    40	                groups = groups.zip(member).map(|(mut source, member)| { source.extend(member); source });
    41	            }
    42	            self.receipt_pending_groups.insert(m.target.clone(), groups);
    43	        }
    44	        let dirty = self.receipt_pending_groups[&m.target].as_ref().is_none_or(|keys|
    45	            group.map_or(!keys.is_empty(), |group| keys.iter().any(|key| key == group)));
    46	        if dirty {
    47	            self.value_source_incomplete = true;
    48	            self.components.note(|frame| frame.pending = true);
    49	        }
    50	        Ok(dirty)
    51	    }
    52	
    53	    fn receipt_overlay_groups(&self, table: &str, fields: &[String], computed: bool) -> Result<Option<Vec<Vec<Value>>>, RuntimeError> {
    54	        if !self.tx.has_provisional(table)? { return Ok(Some(Vec::new())); }
    55	        if computed || fields.iter().any(|f| self.schema.receipt_dependencies.get(table).is_some_and(|owned| owned.contains(f))) {
    56	            return Ok(None);
    57	        }
    58	        self.tx.provisional_groups(table, fields)
    59	    }
    60	
    61	    // Recover a read's parent/group from the pinned index. A member point uses
    62	    // one ordinary-key lookup; a scan without the parent stays conservative.
    63	    pub(super) fn receipt_read_group(&mut self, m: &Maintain, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> Result<Option<Vec<Value>>, RuntimeError> {
    64	        if !self.defer_receipt_maintain(m) { return Ok(None); }
    65	        let columns = self.schema.tables[&m.target].indexes[index].columns();
    66	        let pinned = |field: &str| columns.iter().position(|c| c == field).and_then(|i| key.get(i)).cloned();
    67	        if let Some(k) = &m.row_key {
    68	            if let Some(parent) = pinned(&k.member_key) { return Ok(Some(vec![parent])); }
    69	            if let Some(id) = pinned("member").or_else(|| pinned("id")) {
    70	                let row = self.metered_scan(|r, scope| r.tx.get(scope, &k.table, id.as_str()?))?;
    71	                return Ok(row.and_then(|row| row.field(&k.member_key).ok().cloned()).map(|v| vec![v]));
    72	            }
    73	            return Ok(None);
    74	        }
    75	        Ok(m.by.iter().map(|f| pinned(f)).collect())
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    pub(super) fn maintain(&mut self, source: &str, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    79	        self.receipt_pending_groups.clear();
    80	        // Inserts ordinarily charge their actual aggregate work. A deferred
    81	        // predicate cannot select that work on a device, so use the same public
    82	        // worst-case reservation as update/delete before skipping it. Already
    83	        // prepaid writes must not pay twice.
    84	        if self.device && !self.maintain_prepaid && self.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|
    85	            m.source == source && m.private.is_none() && m.row_key.is_none() && (self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target) || self.schema.tables[&m.target].sync.is_none())) {
    86	            self.reserve_maintains(source, &self.schema.tables[source])?;
    87	        }
    88	        self.maintain_row_views(source, old, new)?;
    89	        let maintains = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|maintain| maintain.source == source && maintain.row_key.is_none());
    90	        for maintain in maintains {
    91	            if self.defer_receipt_maintain(maintain) || (self.device && self.schema.tables[&maintain.target].sync.is_none()) { continue; }
    92	            let source_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]);
    93	            let target = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.target];
    94	            let bytes = source_bytes.saturating_mul(2).saturating_add(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64));
    95	            let prepaid = self.maintain_prepaid;
    96	            if maintain.private.is_some() && self.column_prepaid { self.maintain_prepaid = true; }
    97	            let result = self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| runner.maintain_one(maintain, old, new));
    98	            self.maintain_prepaid = prepaid;
    99	            result?;
   100	        }
   101	        Ok(())
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — reserve interpreted images, both keys, targets and index buffers before allocation; release transient ownership on every exit.
   105	    pub(super) fn maintain_scratch<T>(&mut self, bytes: u64, work: impl FnOnce(&mut Self) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
   106	        if self.maintain_prepaid { return work(self); }
   107	        self.meter.heap(bytes)?;
   108	        let result = work(self);
   109	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = self.meter.used.heap_bytes.saturating_sub(bytes);
   110	        result
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    fn maintain_one(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   114	        let table = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   115	        let mut changes: Vec<(Vec<Value>, i128)> = Vec::new();
   116	        for (image, direction) in [(old, -1), (new, 1)] {
   117	            let Some(image) = image else { continue };
   118	            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   119	            let image = crate::schema::row_image(image, table);
   120	            if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   121	            let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   122	            let change = delta(maintain, &image, direction)?;
   123	            if let Some(existing) = changes.iter_mut().find(|(k, _)| *k == key) { existing.1 += change; }
   124	            else { changes.push((key, change)); }
   125	        }
   126	        for (key, change) in changes {
   127	            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   128	            if let Some(retiring) = self.retiring.as_mut() {
   129	                let entry = retiring.aggregates.entry((maintain.target.clone(), canonical_json(&key))).or_insert_with(|| (key, 0));
   130	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   131	                continue;
   132	            }
   133	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   134	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   135	            }
   136	        }
   137	        Ok(())
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    /// Apply a retirement's net aggregate changes, one per target row, in
   141	    /// key order. Only a total the commit really ends with can overflow.
   142	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   143	    pub(super) fn apply_retired_aggregates(&mut self, changes: BTreeMap<(String, String), (Vec<Value>, i128)>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   144	        let schema = self.schema;
   145	        for ((target, _), (key, change)) in changes {
   146	            let maintain = schema.maintain_for(&target).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing maintain for {target}")))?;
   147	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   148	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   149	            }
   150	        }
   151	        Ok(())
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   155	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain_reported(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, report: &mut crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   156	        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   157	        self.rebuilt_source_rows = 0;
   158	        self.rebuild_maintain(maintain)?;
   159	        let rows = self.rebuilt_source_rows;
   160	        report.views.push(crate::store::adoption_report::ViewBuild { name: maintain.label(), rows, elapsed: started.elapsed() });
   161	        Ok(())
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    /// Build one aggregate from the rows in the store: the target's rows go,
   165	    /// then every source row contributes once. A schema change that adds or
   166	    /// redefines a `maintain` calls this, so a count over existing rows is
   167	    /// right from its first read (day two, LLP 2000.000 §11.2).
   168	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   169	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   170	        let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   171	        let saved = self.column_rebuild;
   172	        self.column_rebuild |= maintain.private.is_some();
   173	        let result = self.rebuild_maintain_inner(maintain);
   174	        self.column_rebuild = saved;
   175	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   176	        result
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   180	    fn rebuild_maintain_inner(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   181	        if maintain.row_key.is_some() { return self.rebuild_row_view(maintain); }
   182	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   183	        let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   184	        let target_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64);
   185	        self.meter.heap(target_bytes.saturating_mul(256))?;
   186	        loop {
   187	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.target, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   188	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   189	            for (existing, key) in page {
   190	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   191	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   192	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   193	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   194	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   195	            }
   196	        }
   197	        let source = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   198	        self.meter.heap(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(source).saturating_mul(256))?;
   199	        let mut totals: BTreeMap<String, (Vec<Value>, i128)> = BTreeMap::new();
   200	        bounds = Bounds::default();
   201	        loop {
   202	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.source, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   203	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   204	            for (mut image, key) in page {
   205	                self.rebuilt_source_rows += 1;
   206	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   207	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, source);
   208	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   209	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   210	                if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   211	                // @ref LLP 3000.028#6 — a total unique owner key has one contribution; backfill streams bounded pages instead of retaining all lifetime keys in a totals map.
   212	                if maintain.own_keys.is_some() {
   213	                    let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   214	                    self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   215	                    self.apply_aggregate(maintain, maintain.source_key(&image), 1, Some(true))?;
   216	                    self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   217	                    continue;
   218	                }
   219	                self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   220	                let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   221	                let change = delta(maintain, &image, 1)?;
   222	                let entry = match totals.entry(canonical_json(&key)) {
   223	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Occupied(entry) => {
   224	                        self.meter.used.heap_bytes -= target_bytes;
   225	                        entry.into_mut()
   226	                    }
   227	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Vacant(entry) => entry.insert((key, 0)),
   228	                };
   229	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   230	            }
   231	        }
   232	        for (_, (key, change)) in totals {
   233	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   234	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, Some(true))?;
   235	            }
   236	        }
   237	        Ok(())
   238	    }
   239	
   240	    fn live_sum(&self, maintain: &Maintain) -> bool {
   241	        matches!(maintain.kind, MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && self.schema.table(&maintain.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring())
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    /// A zero sum is still a group while source rows remain: one signed
   245	    /// contribution may expire before the other. Keep its candidate row so
   246	    /// a scan, snapshot and offline replica can discover the later live sum.
   247	    fn aggregate_source_present(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   248	        // One returned source image and its encoded key (MemoryRows also materializes count_prefix).
   249	        let bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]).saturating_mul(2);
   250	        self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| {
   251	            let source = &runner.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   252	            let index = maintain.contribution_index();
   253	            if !source.indexes.contains_key(&index) { return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("maintain {} needs its contribution index", maintain.target))); }
   254	            runner.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   255	            let present = runner.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.count_prefix(allowance, &maintain.source, &index, key, 1))? != 0;
   256	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — a local positive witness keeps zero; absence needs complete, currently held source coverage.
   257	            if !present && runner.device && !runner.aggregate_source_complete(maintain, key)? {
   258	                runner.unknown_fact(&maintain.source, &index, key, "zero-sum occupancy");
   259	                return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the zero-sum group's source occupancy is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));
   260	            }
   261	            Ok(present)
   262	        })
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    fn aggregate_source_complete(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   266	        let source = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   267	        if source.sync.as_ref().is_none_or(|sync| sync.horizon.is_some()) || self.schema.maintain_subset_unknown(&maintain.target) { return Ok(false); }
   268	        // The target has the same bare audience keys, but no source horizon.
   269	        if !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_group_key(maintain, key))? { return Ok(false); }
   270	        for (name, index) in &source.indexes {
   271	            let values = index.columns().iter().map_while(|column| maintain.by.iter().position(|field| field == column).map(|n| key[n].clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   272	            if self.retained_group(&maintain.source, name, &values)? { return Ok(false); }
   273	        }
   274	        Ok(true)
   275	    }
   276	
   277	    fn apply_aggregate(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: Vec<Value>, change: i128, source_present: Option<bool>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   278	        if self.schema.timelines.iter().any(|t| t.owns(&maintain.target)) {
   279	            return Err(super::timeline::refusal("E_TIMELINE_OWNED", "timeline-owned tables cannot be aggregate targets; maintain a mapped application table instead"));
   280	        }
   281	        let id = maintained_id(&key);
   282	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   283	        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   284	        let existing = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &maintain.target, &id))?;
   285	        let target_key = target_group_key(maintain, &key);
   286	        if self.device && existing.is_none() && !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key)? {
   287	            self.unknown_fact(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key, "aggregate base");
   288	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the affected aggregate value or its absence is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));
   289	        }
   290	        let old_value = existing.as_ref().and_then(|row| row.field("value").ok()).map(Value::as_int).transpose()?.unwrap_or(0);
   291	        let next = i128::from(old_value) + change;
   292	        if !(I53_MIN as i128..=I53_MAX as i128).contains(&next) {
   293	            return Err(aggregate_overflow(maintain));
   294	        }
   295	        let keep_zero = next == 0 && self.live_sum(maintain) && match source_present {
   296	            Some(present) => present,
   297	            None => self.aggregate_source_present(maintain, &key)?,
   298	        };
   299	        if next == 0 && !keep_zero {
   300	            if let Some(existing) = existing {
   301	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   302	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   303	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   304	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   305	            }
   306	            self.mirror_column(maintain, &key, 0)?;
   307	            return Ok(());
   308	        }
   309	        let mut row = crate::small_map::collect([("id".to_owned(), Value::Id(id.clone())), ("value".to_owned(), Value::Int(next as i64))]);
   310	        for (field, value) in maintain.group_fields().iter().zip(key.iter().cloned()) {
   311	            row.insert(field.clone(), value);
   312	        }
   313	        // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — stores index the one logical empty group by a hidden constant.
   314	        if maintain.by.is_empty() { row.insert(GLOBAL.into(), Value::Bool(true)); }
   315	        let row = Value::Row(row.into());
   316	        if existing.as_ref() == Some(&row) { return Ok(()); }
   317	        let entries = self.tx.put(&maintain.target, target, &row, existing.as_ref())?;
   318	        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   319	        self.row_changed(&maintain.target, existing.as_ref(), Some(&row), Origin::Maintained)?;
   320	        self.mirror_column(maintain, &key, next as i64)
   321	    }
   322	}
   323	
   324	pub(super) fn delta(maintain: &Maintain, source: &Value, direction: i64) -> Result<i128, RuntimeError> {
   325	    Ok(match &maintain.kind {
   326	        MaintainKind::Count => i128::from(direction),
   327	        MaintainKind::Sum { of } => i128::from(direction).checked_mul(i128::from(source.field(of)?.as_int()?)).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?,
   328	    })
   329	}
   330	
   331	// @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — an internal sum names its maintain and column, never a retained contribution or computed value.
   332	pub(super) fn aggregate_overflow(maintain: &Maintain) -> RuntimeError {
   333	    RuntimeError::AggregateOverflow { maintain: maintain.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| maintain.target.clone(), |o|o.table.clone()), column: maintain.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| "value".into(), |o|o.column.clone()) }
   334	}

   337	mod timeline_tests {

   339	    #[test]
   340	    fn timeline_owned_tables_cannot_be_maintain_targets() {
   341	        let schema = crate::engine::timeline_authority::write_tests::schema();
   342	        let mut store = crate::Store::memory().unwrap(); store.install(&schema).unwrap();
   343	        let tx = store.transaction().unwrap();

   348	                kind: MaintainKind::Count, filter: None, computed: Default::default(), residency: Default::default(), readable: None, private: None };
   349	            let result = guard.apply_aggregate(&maintain, vec![Value::Id("reader".into())], 1, None);
   350	            assert_eq!(result.unwrap_err().code(), "E_TIMELINE_OWNED");
   351	        }

     1	//! Point reads own authorization and provisional expiry until their answer emits.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — the ordinary point read is `authorized_point`: row and
     3	// field authorization precede result pricing, under the request's one physical
     4	// allowance. 0.4.2's inherited-aggregate, parent-key and event-slot branches
     5	// (LLP 3000.014 §5, LLP 3000.016 §2.2) sit above that boundary and share it.
     6	use super::{runner::Runner, rules, read::PrefixProbe};
     7	use crate::{error::{RuntimeError, MeterKind}, ir::Read, value::Value};
     8	use std::collections::BTreeMap;
     9	
    10	fn unavailable_aggregate() -> Value { crate::value::object([("value", Value::Null)]) }
    11	
    12	impl Runner<'_> {
    13	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — logical guard facts do not promise that rule
    14	    // expiry traversal short-circuits. Null points have no witness on any
    15	    // replica; reject before occupancy, coverage or lifetime work. Maintained
    16	    // null groups remain real groups, and population equality is unchanged.
    17	    pub(super) fn null_unique_point(&self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> bool {
    18	        key.contains(&Value::Null) && self.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()
    19	            && self.schema.table(table).and_then(|t| t.index(index)).is_some_and(|i| i.unique)
    20	    }
    21	
    22	    pub(super) fn created(&self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> bool {
    23	        if self.null_unique_point(table, index, key) { return false; }
    24	        self.created.contains_key(&(table.to_owned(), index.to_owned(), crate::value::canonical_json(&key)))
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    /// A point read: the row when it exists and the viewer may read it, else
    28	    /// `null`. Hidden and absent are the same word on purpose.
    29	    pub(super) fn get(&mut self, read: &Read) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
    30	        if self.decoration_cache.is_some() { return self.decoration_get(read); }
    31	        let value = self.get_uncached(read)?;
    32	        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    33	        if self.following_witnesses.is_some() && let Value::Row(fields) = &value {
    34	            let bytes = 256 + read.table.len() as u64 + fields.keys().map(|f| f.len() as u64 + 32).sum::<u64>() + value.field("id")?.as_str()?.len() as u64;
    35	            self.meter.heap(bytes)?;
    36	            self.following_witnesses.as_mut().unwrap().push(super::following_history::projection::Witness { table:read.table.clone(),id:value.field("id")?.as_str()?.into(),fields:fields.keys().cloned().collect() });
    37	        }
    38	        Ok(value)
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    pub(super) fn get_uncached(&mut self, read: &Read) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
    42	        let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
    43	        let mut emitted = false;
    44	        self.schema.refuse_private(&read.table)?;
    45	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.21 — an unfinished maintained value is unavailable, never an absent group coerced to zero.
    46	        let maintained = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some();
    47	        if maintained { self.meter.require_uncapped()?; }
    48	        let mut value = self.get_inner(read, &mut emitted)?;
    49	        if maintained { self.meter.require_uncapped()?; }
    50	        if emitted && self.meter.capped_at.is_none() { self.stamp_source(&read.table, &mut value)?; expiry.emit(); }
    51	        Ok(value)
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    fn get_inner(&mut self, read: &Read, emitted: &mut bool) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
    55	        let key = read.key.iter().map(|value| self.expr(value)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
    56	        if self.null_unique_point(&read.table, &read.index, &key) { return Ok(Value::Null); }
    57	        self.observe_sequence_selection(&read.table, &read.index, &key)?;
    58	        let table = self.schema.table(&read.table).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {}", read.table)))?;
    59	        self.read_tables.insert(read.table.clone());
    60	        self.rule_dependencies(&table.rules.read);
    61	        self.meter.at(&read.site);
    62	        self.aggregate_read_coverage(&read.table, &read.index, &key, &read.site)?;
    63	        let Some(maintain) = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table) else {
    64	            let (value, price) = self.get_prepared(read, &key, emitted)?;
    65	            self.meter.heap(price)?;
    66	            return Ok(value);
    67	        };
    68	        // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.4 — a generation-wide acquired flag cannot lend another caller an empty owner partition.
    69	        if self.device && maintain.own_keys.is_some() && !self.tx.current_group(&read.table, &read.index, &key)? {
    70	            self.unknown_fact(&read.table, &read.index, &key, &read.site);
    71	            self.components.note(|frame| frame.speculative = true);
    72	            return Ok(Value::Null);
    73	        }
    74	        if let Some(message) = maintain.expiring_read_refusal(self.schema) {
    75	            return Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_MAINTAIN_READ_EXPIRE", message });
    76	        }
    77	        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a point hit, miss or denial reserves the same reusable row scratch before discovering occupancy.
    78	        let scratch = self.aggregate_point_scratch(maintain, &key);
    79	        self.meter.heap(scratch)?;
    80	        let result = (|| {
    81	            self.read_charge(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
    82	            self.meter.require_uncapped()?;
    83	            self.get_prepared(read, &key, emitted)
    84	        })();
    85	        // Transfer only the sealed result to retained ownership. Keep the
    86	        // high-water mark, work counters and any cut when releasing scratch.
    87	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = self.meter.used.heap_bytes.saturating_sub(scratch);
    88	        let (value, price) = result?;
    89	        self.meter.heap(price)?;
    90	        Ok(value)
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    /// The answer and the sealed price of its retained image, charged by the caller
    94	    /// once its scratch is released.
    95	    fn get_prepared(&mut self, read: &Read, key: &[Value], emitted: &mut bool) -> Result<(Value, u64), RuntimeError> {
    96	        let table = self.schema.table(&read.table).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {}", read.table)))?;
    97	        let index = table.index(&read.index).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing index {}.{}", read.table, read.index)))?;
    98	        let maintained = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table);
    99	        let inherited = maintained.filter(|m| self.schema.tables[&m.source].through.is_some());
   100	        let mut key_admitted = false;
   101	        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — only key-determined inputs may authorize a synthetic aggregate row.
   102	        if let Some(m) = inherited.filter(|m| m.parent_key.is_some() || rules::deciding_fields(&read.table, table, &table.rules.read).iter().all(|field| index.columns().iter().take(key.len()).any(|column| column == field) || (field == "id" && m.group_fields().iter().all(|f| index.columns().iter().take(key.len()).any(|column| column == f))))) {
   103	            let visible = (|| Ok(if let Some(parent) = &m.parent_key {
   104	                let Some(at) = index.columns().iter().position(|f| f == &parent.reference) else { return Err(RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_READ_THROUGH_SCAN",message:format!("{} must be read by its full parent key; use {}[parent] (guide maintains)",read.table,read.table)}); };
   105	                let parent_id = key.get(at).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("read through aggregate needs its full parent key".into()))?;
   106	                match self.parent_verdict(&m.source,parent,parent_id)? {
   107	                    super::through::ParentVerdict::Readable => true,
   108	                    super::through::ParentVerdict::Unknown => { self.unknown_fact(&read.table,&read.index,key,&read.site); false },
   109	                    _ => false,
   110	                }
   111	            } else {
   112	                let mut fields: BTreeMap<_, _> = crate::small_map::collect(index.columns().into_iter().zip(key.iter().cloned()));
   113	                let group_fields = m.group_fields();
   114	                if group_fields.iter().all(|f| fields.contains_key(f)) {
   115	                    let group: Vec<_> = group_fields.iter().map(|f| fields[f].clone()).collect();
   116	                    fields.insert("id".into(), Value::Id(crate::schema::maintained_id(&group)));
   117	                }
   118	                let row = Value::Row(fields.into());
   119	                self.meter.charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read))?;
   120	                let viewer = self.context.viewer.clone();
   121	                if self.device { self.authorize_read(&read.table, &row, true)? }
   122	                else if self.context.is_system() { true }
   123	                else {
   124	                    // The key rule's probes were charged above, possibly to a cut; its
   125	                    // witnesses still run under the request's physical allowance and
   126	                    // the cut stops the read before any aggregate state is fetched.
   127	                    self.prepaid_witnesses(|runner, allowance| rules::evaluate(&table.rules.read,&viewer,None,&row,false,&mut PrefixProbe { runner, allowance }))?
   128	                }
   129	            }))();
   130	            if !visible? || self.read_capped() { return Ok((unavailable_aggregate(), 0)); }
   131	            key_admitted = true;
   132	            if self.device && (table.residency.online_only || (m.parent_key.is_none() && !self.captured_aggregate_coverage(m, index, key)?)) {
   133	                self.unknown_fact(&read.table, &read.index, key, &read.site);
   134	                return Ok((unavailable_aggregate(), 0));
   135	            }
   136	            if self.device && m.parent_key.is_some() {
   137	                // A locally synthesized total has no settled row to freeze.
   138	                // @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — never recount provisional receipt inputs.
   139	                let group = self.receipt_read_group(m, &read.index, key)?;
   140	                if self.beyond_horizon || self.receipt_maintain_pending(m, group.as_deref())? { return Ok((unavailable_aggregate(), 0)); }
   141	                // The parent-key view is derived locally from fully held children;
   142	                // its generated table need not have an invertible sync audience.
   143	                let fields = crate::small_map::collect(index.columns().into_iter().zip(key.iter().cloned()));
   144	                let row = self.live_aggregate_inner(m, Value::Row(fields.into()))?.unwrap_or(Value::Null);
   145	                *emitted = true;
   146	                // Key-authorized and computed from the caller's key and the disclosed total: sealed without a lookup.
   147	                let sealed = self.authorized_synthetic(table, row);
   148	                let price = sealed.heap_bytes() + sealed.projection_heap() + sealed.materialization_heap();
   149	                return Ok((sealed.into_value(), price));
   150	            }
   151	        }
   152	        if self.filter_depth > 0 && !self.read_charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, 1)? { return Ok((Value::Null, 0)); }
   153	        if self.device && table.sync.is_none() {
   154	            self.unknown_fact(&read.table, &read.index, key, &read.site);
   155	            self.components.note(|frame| frame.speculative = true);
   156	            self.withhold_unheld_card(&read.table);
   157	        }
   158	        if key.iter().any(|value| *value == Value::Null) && !index.columns().iter().zip(key).all(|(column, value)| *value != Value::Null || table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| kind.is_optional())) {
   159	            // A null key on a required column addresses nothing.
   160	            return Ok((Value::Null, 0));
   161	        }
   162	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — reserve authorization before knowing hit, miss or hidden; an exhausted probe is not a false verdict to roll back.
   163	        let trusted = key_admitted || self.context.is_system() || self.trust_synced_read(table);
   164	        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — prediction keeps the server's reservation even for
   165	        // delivered rows; a key-admitted aggregate has already paid its rule.
   166	        let prepaid = !self.context.is_system() && !key_admitted;
   167	        if prepaid && !self.read_charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read))? { return Ok((Value::Null, 0)); }
   168	        // @ref LLP 3000.013#14.7 — the requested key authorizes a
   169	        // missing aggregate before its logical zero is disclosed. A hidden hit
   170	        // and hidden miss are the same unavailable value.
   171	        let authorize_missing_group = maintained.is_some() && !key_admitted;
   172	        let tables_mark = self.read_tables.clone();
   173	        let mut absent = false;
   174	        let found = self.authorized_point(read, index, key, prepaid, trusted, maintained.is_some(), &mut absent)?;
   175	        let Some(row) = found else {
   176	            if absent && authorize_missing_group {
   177	                // The same reservation owns a live row or a logical zero.
   178	                let mut fields: BTreeMap<_, _> = crate::small_map::collect(index.columns().into_iter().zip(key.iter().cloned()));
   179	                let m = maintained.expect("maintained aggregate");
   180	                if !m.by.iter().all(|field| fields.contains_key(field)) { return Ok((unavailable_aggregate(), 0)); }
   181	                let group: Vec<_> = m.by.iter().map(|field| fields[field].clone()).collect();
   182	                fields.insert("id".into(), Value::Id(crate::schema::maintained_id(&group)));
   183	                fields.insert("value".into(), Value::Int(0));
   184	                let zero = Value::Row(fields.into());
   185	                // The rule was prepaid above; its witnesses run on the restoring
   186	                // scope. This is a synthetic image, so a device must evaluate its
   187	                // key rule rather than trust delivery of a row it never received.
   188	                let authorized = self.authorized_scratch(0, &[], |runner, _| {
   189	                    if runner.context.is_system() || runner.authorize_read(&read.table, &zero, true)? {
   190	                        Ok(Some(runner.authorized_synthetic(table, zero)))
   191	                    } else { Ok(None) }
   192	                })?;
   193	                if self.read_capped() { return Ok((Value::Null, 0)); }
   194	                return Ok(match authorized {
   195	                    Some(row) => { self.next_expiry.inherit(row.expiry()); *emitted = true; (Value::Null, 0) }
   196	                    None => { self.read_tables = tables_mark; (unavailable_aggregate(), 0) }
   197	                });
   198	            }
   199	            if absent {
   200	                // An authorized missing aggregate emits its logical zero: null here,
   201	                // which the compiled read coerces to 0 (LLP 3000.007 §20.21).
   202	                *emitted = key_admitted;
   203	                return Ok((Value::Null, 0));
   204	            }
   205	            self.read_tables = tables_mark;
   206	            return Ok((if maintained.is_some() { unavailable_aggregate() } else { Value::Null }, 0));
   207	        };
   208	        let price = row.heap_bytes() + row.projection_heap() + row.materialization_heap();
   209	        let mut value = row.into_value();
   210	        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5 — retained history stays marked through projections; ordinary retained
   211	        // point reads keep their established component composition (the row-level fallback is read-through's).
   212	        if self.device && table.through.is_some() && self.context.row_provenance && self.retained_row(&read.table, &value)? {
   213	            if let Value::Row(fields) = &mut value { fields.mark_retained(); }
   214	        }
   215	        let value = self.sequence_read_result(&read.table, value)?;
   216	        *emitted = true;
   217	        Ok((if matches!(self.timeline_authority, Some(super::timeline_authority::Authority::Transition(_))) { value }
   218	            else { super::recorded::public_image(table, value) }, price))
   219	    }
   220	}
  1040	    Ok((read.table.clone(), index.name, ordered, equalities))
  1041	}
  1042	
  1043	fn title(value: &str) -> String {
  1044	    let mut chars = value.chars();
  1045	    match chars.next() {
  1046	        Some(first) => first.to_uppercase().collect::<String>() + chars.as_str(),
  1047	        None => String::new(),
  1048	    }
  1049	}
  1050	
  1051	/// The columns of a generated maintain target: `id`, `value`, and the `by`
  1052	/// columns typed as the source declares them.
  1053	pub fn maintain_target(source: &TableInfo, maintain: &MaintainDecl) -> Fallible<TableDecl> {
  1054	    const GLOBAL: &str = "$global";
  1055	    let site = maintain.site.clone();
  1056	    if source.maintained.is_some() { return Err(super::maintains::refusal(&site, "a maintain cannot use another maintain as its source", "define the view over application source rows, or use a job sweep")); }
  1057	    let mut columns = Vec::new();
  1058	    let value_type = match &maintain.column {
  1059	        None => ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Int, optional: false },
  1060	        Some(column) => match source.columns.get(column) {
  1061	            Some(ty) if ty.optional && matches!(ty.kind, TypeKind::Int | TypeKind::IntRange { .. } | TypeKind::Decimal { .. } | TypeKind::Money { .. }) => {
  1062	                return Err(type_error(&site, format!("sum over {}.{column} cannot add null: the numeric column is optional (`?`); its numeric range is accepted", maintain.source),
  1063	                    format!("make {column} required (`{column}: {}`) and give every row a numeric contribution; if absence must stay distinct, add a required numeric contribution column default 0 and sum that column instead", crate::print::scalar_type(&ty.required()))).guide("maintains"));
  1064	            }
  1065	            Some(ty) if matches!(ty.kind, TypeKind::IntRange { .. }) => ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Int, optional: false },
  1066	            Some(ty) if matches!(ty.kind, TypeKind::Int | TypeKind::Decimal { .. } | TypeKind::Money { .. }) => ty.required(),
  1067	            Some(ty) => return Err(type_error(&site, format!("sum over {}.{column} needs int, decimal or money, not {}", maintain.source, crate::print::scalar_type(ty)), format!("view {}: count {} by {}", maintain.name, maintain.source, maintain.keys.iter().map(|key| super::maintains::bare_key(key).map_or_else(|| crate::print::expression(key), |name| format!(".{name}"))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")))),
  1068	            None => return Err(unknown(&site, format!("sum names unknown column {}.{column}", maintain.source), name_rewrite(column, source.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None))),
  1069	        },
  1070	    };
  1071	    columns.push(ColumnDecl { on_delete: None, sequence: None, written_by: None, readable_by_audience: false, maintained: None, default: None, name: "value".into(), type_: value_type, site: site.clone() });
  1072	    let computed = super::maintains::keys(&source, maintain)?;
  1073	    let aliases = super::maintains::inherited_aliases(source,maintain);
  1074	    if let Some(expr) = &maintain.filter { super::maintains::predicate(&source, expr)?; }
  1075	    for field in &maintain.by {
  1076	        let ty = if computed.contains_key(field) { ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Int, optional: computed[field].column.contains('.') } } else { source.columns[aliases.get(field).unwrap_or(field)].clone() };
  1077	        columns.push(ColumnDecl { on_delete: None, sequence: None, written_by: None, readable_by_audience: false, maintained: None, default: None, name: field.clone(), type_: ty, site: site.clone() });
  1078	    }
  1079	    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — the logical empty tuple gets one store-indexable, compiler-only discriminator.
  1080	    let group = if maintain.by.is_empty() {
  1081	        columns.push(ColumnDecl { on_delete: None, sequence: None, written_by: None, readable_by_audience: false, maintained: None, default: None, name: GLOBAL.into(), type_: ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Bool, optional: false }, site: site.clone() });
  1082	        vec![GLOBAL.into()]
  1083	    } else { maintain.by.clone() };
  1084	    let mut indexes = vec![IndexDecl { name: "byGroup".into(), columns: group, unique: true, refusal: None, words: None, computed: BTreeMap::new(), site: site.clone() }];
  1085	    let mut names = BTreeSet::from(["byGroup".to_owned(), "byId".to_owned()]);
  1086	    for index in &maintain.indexes {
  1087	        if !names.insert(index.name.clone()) { return Err(super::maintains::refusal(&index.site, format!("duplicate maintain index {}", index.name), "use a distinct index name; byGroup and byId are generated")); }
  1088	        let index = index.clone();
  1089	        if index.columns.contains(&"value".into()) {
  1090	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.21 — stored order is stale; the sound server count upper-bound threshold read is not built.
  1091	            if source.decl.expire.is_some() || source.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.expiring) { return Err(Diagnostic::new(&index.site, "E_QUARRY_MAINTAIN_EXPIRE", format!("maintain {} cannot order by value because source {} expires: stored order is stale before the physical sweep, so taking stored top-N can miss a live winner; a count upper-bound threshold scan is not built", maintain.name, maintain.source), "for an entity-time window over a non-expiring source, use the Trending predicate in guide feeds; for expiring contributions, stream each candidate's live count and rank only a complete candidate domain; a meter cut makes the ranking incomplete").guide("feeds")); }
  1092	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#14 — ruling 41's interim retires with maintained columns.
  1093	            let value_at = index.columns.iter().position(|field| field == "value").unwrap();
  1094	            let fields = index.columns.iter().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
  1095	            if !index.is_plain() || fields.len() != index.columns.len()
  1096	                || index.columns[value_at..] != ["value", "id"]
  1097	                || index.columns[..value_at].iter().any(|field| !maintain.by.contains(field)) {
  1098	                return Err(super::maintains::refusal(&index.site,
  1099	                    "a maintain value index is a prefix of group fields, then value, id; fields cannot repeat",
  1100	                    "end this index with value, id; for post-time ties declare likeCount: count likes on posts, then read posts last 20 by .likeCount, .at after c (guide maintains)"));
  1101	            }
  1102	        }
  1103	        indexes.push(index);
  1104	    }
  1105	    Ok(TableDecl {
  1106	        receipts: None,
  1107	        ephemeral: None,
  1108	        search: Vec::new(),
  1109	        name: maintain.name.clone(),
  1110	        columns,
  1111	        indexes,
  1112	        rules: maintain.read.iter().cloned().collect(),
  1113	        immutable: Vec::new(),
  1114	        caps: Vec::new(),
  1115	        final_exists: Vec::new(),
  1116	        retain: None,
  1117	        public: None,
  1118	        sync: None,
  1119	        online_only: maintain.online_only,
  1120	        not_before: None,
  1121	        expire: None,
  1122	        site,
  1123	    })
  1124	}
  1125	
  1126	#[cfg(test)]
  1127	mod tests {
  1128	    #[test]
  1129	    fn quarry_refuses_nullable_target_sync_group_keys() {
  1130	        let source = r#"
  1131	table memberships:
  1132	  room: id ?
  1133	  principal: principal
  1134	  unique byRoomPrincipal: room, principal
  1135	  by byPrincipalRoom: principal, room, id
  1136	  read <- .principal = viewer
  1137	table messages:
  1138	  room: id
  1139	  body: text <=100
  1140	  by byRoom: room, id
  1141	  read <- exists memberships[.room, viewer]
  1142	  sync to memberships[.room]
  1143	"#;
  1144	        let refused = crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
  1145	        assert!(refused.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_SYNC" && diagnostic.guide == "sync" && diagnostic.message.contains("needs required group keys") && diagnostic.rewrite.contains("remove `?`")), "{:?}", refused.diagnostics);
  1146	        let accepted = crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.replace("room: id ?", "room: id"))]);
  1147	        assert!(accepted.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", accepted.diagnostics);
  1148	    }
  1149	
  1150	    // LLP 3000.036 §1 A: the rule in source form, the paved split, and no "make it public".
  1151	    #[test]
  1152	    fn e_sync_quotes_the_source_rule_and_names_the_split() {
  1153	        let sync = |rule: &str, sync: &str| {
  1154	            let source = format!("table claims:\n  claimant: principal\n  postedBy: principal\n  approved: bool\n  read <- {rule}\n  insert <- .postedBy = viewer\n  {sync}\n");
  1155	            let bound = crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
  1156	            bound.diagnostics.into_iter().find(|d| d.code == "E_SYNC").expect("E_SYNC")
  1157	        };
  1158	        // @ref LLP 3000.036.000.000#2.1 — two parties are a union now; naming one of them is a disagreement whose rewrite is the union.
  1159	        let two = sync(".claimant = viewer or .postedBy = viewer", "sync to .claimant");
  1160	        assert!(two.message.contains("`read <- .claimant = viewer or .postedBy = viewer`"), "{}", two.message);
  1161	        assert!(two.rewrite.contains("`sync to .claimant or .postedBy`"), "{}", two.rewrite);
  1162	        let source = "table claims:\n  claimant: principal\n  postedBy: principal\n  read <- .claimant = viewer or .postedBy = viewer\n  insert <- .postedBy = viewer\n  sync\n";
  1163	        assert!(crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]).diagnostics.is_empty());
  1164	        // A disjunct that names no reader is still refused with the split.
  1165	        let partial = sync("(.claimant = viewer and .approved) or .postedBy = viewer", "sync");
  1166	        assert!(partial.message.contains("do not make private rows public"), "{}", partial.message);
  1167	        let shared = sync(".approved or .postedBy = viewer", "sync");
  1168	        assert!(shared.message.contains("`read <- .approved or .postedBy = viewer`") && !shared.message.contains("Bool"), "{}", shared.message);
  1169	        assert!(shared.rewrite.contains("`public '..'` table") && shared.rewrite.contains("`.postedBy = viewer`"), "{}", shared.rewrite);
  1170	        // The split the rewrite names compiles.
  1171	        let split = "table claims:\n  claimant: principal\n  postedBy: principal\n  read <- exists claimsParties[.id, viewer]\n  insert <- .postedBy = viewer\n  after insert <- exists claimsParties[item = .id, party = .claimant]\n  after insert <- exists claimsParties[item = .id, party = .postedBy]\n  sync\ntable claimsParties:\n  item: claims\n  party: principal\n  unique item, party\n  immutable item, party\n  read <- exists claimsParties[.item, viewer]\n  insert <- created claims[.item]\n  sync\n";
  1172	        let bound = crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), split.into())]);
  1173	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().all(|d| !d.code.starts_with("E_")), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
  1174	    }
  1175	}

   130	pub const PRESENCE_CONFIRMATION: std::time::Duration = std::time::Duration::from_secs(5);
   131	/// How soon a client re-runs a live query that read `connected[p]`. Presence
   132	/// changes without a commit, so no change feed names it (LLP 3000.018 §3).
   133	pub const PRESENCE_REFRESH: std::time::Duration = std::time::Duration::from_secs(5);
   134	
   135	/// Who is connected right now: what a server knows from the long polls it
   136	/// holds open, answered to a program's `connected[p]`. Only a server has
   137	/// one; a device treats every principal as connected and lets the server
   138	/// decide (`Runner::presence_unknown`).
   139	pub trait Presence: std::fmt::Debug + Send + Sync {
   140	    /// How many connections `principal` holds open now.
   141	    fn connections(&self, principal: &str) -> u64;
   142	    /// A single observation: (live connections, grace deadline in engine time).
   143	    fn effect_presence(&self, principal: &str) -> (u64, Option<i64>) { (self.connections(principal), None) }
   144	    /// Pin an unconfirmed poll's deadline to the mutation's commit clock.
   145	    fn effect_presence_at(&self, principal: &str, now: i64) -> (u64, Option<i64>) {
   146	        let (live, deadline) = self.effect_presence(principal);
   147	        (live, if live > 0 { Some(now.saturating_add(PRESENCE_CONFIRMATION.as_millis() as i64)) } else { deadline },
   148	        )
   149	    }
   150	}

   155	/// Who runs the program, with what, and when.
   156	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
   157	pub struct Context {
   158	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   159	    pub(crate) refusal_trace: Option<refusal_trace::RefusalTrace>,
   160	    pub retained_search: bool,
   161	    /// Bounded retained ordering keys supplied by the coordinator, never authority.
   162	    #[cfg(feature = "server-search")]
   163	    pub search_retained_order: Option<(Vec<i64>, bool)>,
   164	    #[cfg(feature = "server-search")]
   165	    pub(crate) search_retained_candidates: Vec<(String, i64, bool)>,
   166	    pub read_spent: Option<crate::meter::Metered>,
   167	    pub viewer: String,
   168	    pub kind: String,
   169	    pub args: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
   170	    pub now: Option<i64>,
   171	    pub read_time: Option<i64>,
   172	    pub(crate) row_provenance: bool,
   173	    /// Digest only: the core resolves it in the admission transaction.
   174	    pub(crate) session_digest: Option<String>,
   175	    /// Original HTTP arguments, before typed decoding or default insertion.
   176	    pub wire_args: Option<serde_json::Value>,
   177	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   178	    pub delivery_fingerprint: Option<String>,
   179	    pub(crate) generation: u64,
   180	    /// Ids the caller minted for this run, consumed in order by `new`.
   181	    pub new_ids: VecDeque<String>,
   182	    entropy: u64,
   183	    /// Every id consumed by this execution, including newly minted ids.
   184	    pub(crate) minted_ids: Vec<String>,
   185	    /// The server's live registry behind `connected[p]`, when there is one.
   186	    pub presence: Option<std::sync::Arc<dyn Presence>>,
   187	}
   188	
   189	
   190	impl Context {
   191	    pub fn new(viewer: impl Into<String>, args: BTreeMap<String, Value>) -> Self {
   192	        Self { #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] refusal_trace: None, #[cfg(feature = "server-search")] search_retained_order: None, #[cfg(feature = "server-search")] search_retained_candidates: Vec::new(), retained_search: false, read_spent: None, viewer: viewer.into(), kind: "user".into(), args, now: None, read_time: None, row_provenance: false, session_digest: None, wire_args: None, #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] delivery_fingerprint: None, generation: 0, new_ids: VecDeque::new(), entropy: 0, minted_ids: Vec::new(), presence: None }
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   196	    pub fn with_session(mut self, token: &str) -> Self {
   197	        self.session_digest = Some(crate::identity::digest(token));
   198	        self
   199	    }
   200	
   201	    /// Developer host instrumentation; never accepted from wire arguments.
   202	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   203	    pub fn with_refusal_trace(mut self, trace: refusal_trace::RefusalTrace) -> Self {
   204	        self.refusal_trace = Some(trace); self
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    /// Request the metered, output-only source-identity envelope for a query.
   208	    pub fn with_row_provenance(mut self) -> Self { self.row_provenance = true; self }
   209	
   210	    pub fn with_wire_args(mut self, args: serde_json::Value) -> Self {
   211	        self.wire_args = Some(args);
   212	        self
   213	    }
   214	
   215	    /// The host supplies its active backend generation for opaque page cursors.
   216	    pub fn with_generation(mut self, generation: u64) -> Self { self.generation = generation; self }
   217	
   218	    pub fn with_presence(mut self, presence: Option<std::sync::Arc<dyn Presence>>) -> Self {
   219	        self.presence = presence;
   220	        self
   221	    }
   222	
   223	    pub fn system() -> Self {
   224	        let mut context = Self::new(crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, BTreeMap::new());
   225	        context.kind = "system".into();
   226	        context
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    pub fn at(mut self, now: i64) -> Self {
   230	        self.now = Some(now);
   231	        self
   232	    }
   233	
   234	    pub fn with_ids(mut self, ids: impl IntoIterator<Item = String>) -> Self {
   235	        self.new_ids = ids.into_iter().collect();
   236	        self
   237	    }
   238	
   239	    pub fn with_entropy(mut self, entropy: u64) -> Self {
   240	        self.entropy = entropy;
   241	        self
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    pub fn is_system(&self) -> bool {
   245	        self.viewer == crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL
   246	    }
   247	
   248	    pub(crate) fn mint(&mut self) -> Result<String, crate::RuntimeError> {
   249	        let id = if let Some(id) = self.new_ids.pop_front() { id } else {
   250	            if self.entropy == 0 { self.entropy = crate::ids::seed(); }
   251	            crate::ids::mint(self.now.unwrap_or(0), &mut self.entropy)?
   252	        };
   253	        self.minted_ids.push(id.clone());
   254	        Ok(id)
   255	    }
   256	}

   258	/// Disclosed cut reason; non-uniform server reads withhold it. Sites and
   259	/// counters remain operator detail.
   260	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   261	#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
   262	pub enum ReadCut { Meter, Words }
   263	
   264	// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22.7 — held search stays on the device; server search uses the grouped or newest form.
   265	#[cfg(any(feature = "sqlite", feature = "postgres"))]
   266	pub(crate) fn server_query_allowed(schema: &crate::schema::Schema, name: &str) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
   267	    if schema.operations.get(name).is_some_and(|fact| fact.held) {
   268	        return Err(Refusal::new("E_QUERY_HELD", "residency", format!("query {name} is held: it runs only on the device"))
   269	            .rewrite("call this query with the local client; for server search, use the grouped or newest form in `snapback4 guide search`, section \"Search every chat I'm in\"")
   270	            .guide("search"));
   271	    }
   272	    Ok(())
   273	}
