    85	fn rebuild_groups(next: &str, remaining: usize) {
    86	    let mut e = groups();
    87	    let next = compile(next);
    88	    let estimate = preview(&mut e, &next.schema);
    89	    assert_eq!(estimate.retired_rows, 4);
    90	    assert!(estimate.retired_index_entries >= 8);
    91	    assert!(estimate.summary().contains("unestimated retirement pause"));
    92	    e.adopt_at(next.schema, next.programs, 10).unwrap();
    93	    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("totals").unwrap().len(), remaining);

    96	#[test]
    97	fn missing_identity_marker_rebuilds_a_principal_reference_and_max_text_group() {
    98	    const SOURCE: &str = "table parents:\n  public\ntable entries:\n  owner: principal\n  parent: parents\n  token: text <=64\n  amount: int\n  public\nview totals: sum entries of .amount by .owner, .parent, .token\n  public\n";
    99	    let initial = compile(SOURCE);
   100	    let mut e = Engine::new_at(Store::memory().unwrap(), initial.schema, initial.programs, 0).unwrap();
   101	    let owner = format!("user:{}", "o".repeat(59));
   102	    let parent = "p".repeat(64);
   103	    let token = "t".repeat(64);
   104	    assert_eq!((owner.len(), parent.len(), token.len()), (64, 64, 64));
   105	    e.seed_at([
   106	        ("parents".into(), object([("id", Value::Id(parent.clone()))])),
   107	        ("entries".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("entry".into())), ("owner", Value::Id(owner.clone())),
   108	            ("parent", Value::Id(parent.clone())), ("token", Value::String(token.clone())), ("amount", Value::Int(7))])),
   109	    ], 0).unwrap();
   110	    let expected = crate::schema::maintained_id(&[Value::Id(owner), Value::Id(parent), Value::String(token)]);
   111	    let before = e.store().rows("totals").unwrap();
   112	    assert_eq!(before.len(), 1);
   113	    assert_eq!(before[0].field("id").unwrap(), &Value::Id(expected.clone()));
   114	    assert_eq!(before[0].field("value").unwrap(), &Value::Int(7));
   115	    assert_eq!(expected.len(), 64);
   116	
   117	    e.store().conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key=?1", [crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META]).unwrap();
   118	    let next = compile(SOURCE);
   119	    assert_eq!(preview(&mut e, &next.schema).aggregate_rows, 1);
   120	    let generation = e.generation();
   121	    e.adopt_at(next.schema, next.programs, 1).unwrap();
   122	    assert!(e.generation() > generation);
   123	    assert_eq!(e.store().meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META).unwrap().as_deref(), Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION));
   124	    let after = e.store().rows("totals").unwrap();
   125	    assert_eq!(after.len(), 1);
   126	    assert_eq!(after[0].field("id").unwrap(), &Value::Id(expected));
   127	    assert_eq!(after[0].field("value").unwrap(), &Value::Int(7));
   128	}

    69	impl Store {
    70	    pub fn adoption_estimate(
    71	        &mut self,
    72	        schema: &Schema,
    73	    ) -> Result<Option<AdoptionEstimate>, RuntimeError> {
    74	        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
    75	        let previous: Option<String> = tx.tx.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')", [], |r| r.get(0)).optional()?;
    76	        let Some(previous) = previous else {
    77	            return Ok(None);
    78	        };
    79	        let previous: Schema = crate::json_codec::from_text(&previous)
    80	            .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
    81	        let changed = previous != *schema;
    82	        // Legacy live-sum repair can rebuild retained targets even with no schema delta.
    83	        let repair_sums = tx.meta("live_sum_candidates")?.as_deref() != Some("1");
    84	        let repair_maintained_ids = schema.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())
    85	            && tx.meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META)?.as_deref() != Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
    86	        let cap_schema = tx.meta("cap_counts_schema")?;
    87	        let rebuild_caps = (crate::rows::caps::required(schema) || cap_schema.is_some())
    88	            && cap_schema.as_deref() != Some(&schema.hash());
    89	        let rebuild: Vec<_> = schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|
    90	            (changed && crate::successor::maintain_rebuild_needed(&previous, schema, m))
    91	                || (repair_maintained_ids && m.row_key.is_none())
    92	                || (repair_sums && matches!(m.kind, crate::schema::MaintainKind::Sum { .. })
    93	                    && schema.table(&m.source).is_some_and(|t| t.is_expiring()))).collect();
    94	        if !changed && rebuild.is_empty() && !rebuild_caps {
    95	            return Ok(None);
    96	        }
    97	        let plans = super::activation_plan::plans(schema, Some(&previous));
    98	        let mut counts = BTreeMap::<String, u64>::new();
    99	        let mut count = |name: &str| -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   100	            if let Some(count) = counts.get(name) {
   101	                return Ok(*count);
   102	            }
   103	            let rows = if let Some(t) = tx.catalog.get(name) {
   104	                tx.tx
   105	                    .query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM r WHERE t = ?1", [t], |r| {
   106	                        r.get::<_, i64>(0)
   107	                    })? as u64
   108	            } else {
   109	                0
   110	            };
   111	            counts.insert(name.to_owned(), rows);
   112	            Ok(rows)
   113	        };
   114	        let mut estimate = AdoptionEstimate {
   115	            cache_mib: super::activation_cache::configured_mib()?,
   116	            ..Default::default()
   117	        };
   118	        // Count each predecessor physical population once, including surviving
   119	        // names cleared by timeline retirement, index replacement and rebuilds.
   120	        let active = super::activation_plan::active_names(schema);
   121	        let mut retired: std::collections::BTreeSet<String> = tx.catalog.keys()
   122	            .filter(|name| !active.contains(*name)).cloned().collect();
   123	        let mut cleared = crate::successor::retired_timeline_tables(&previous, schema);
   124	        for m in &rebuild {
   125	            cleared.insert(m.target.clone());
   126	            if m.row_key.is_some() {
   127	                cleared.extend([m.work_table(), format!("$basis:{}", m.target)]);
   128	            }
   129	        }
   130	        for name in cleared {
   131	            retired.insert(name.clone());
   132	            for table in previous.table(&name).into_iter().chain(schema.table(&name)) {
   133	                retired.extend(table.indexes.keys().map(|index| format!("{name}.{index}")));
   134	            }
   135	        }
   136	        for (name, plan) in &plans {
   137	            retired.extend(plan.indexes.iter().map(|index| format!("{name}.{index}")));
   138	        }
   139	        for num in retired.iter().filter_map(|name| tx.catalog.get(name)) {
   140	            let (rows, indexes): (i64, i64) = tx.tx.query_row(
   141	                "SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM r WHERE t=?1), (SELECT count(*) FROM i WHERE ti=?1)",
   142	                [num], |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)))?;
   143	            estimate.retired_rows = estimate.retired_rows.saturating_add(rows as u64);
   144	            estimate.retired_index_entries = estimate.retired_index_entries.saturating_add(indexes as u64);
   145	        }
   146	        // Selection is clock-independent. Affected tables may retire old rows;
   147	        // unaffected through tables still rewrite every row's deadline. Avoid
   148	        // predicting how many rows expire at the later activation's pinned time.
   149	        let through = crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::new(schema, &previous, 0);
   150	        for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
   151	            if changed && (through.affects(name) || table.through.is_some()) {
   152	                let rows = count(name)?;
   153	                if rows != 0 {
   154	                    let label = super::adoption_report::table_label(schema, name);
   155	                    if through.affects(name) {
   156	                        estimate.unestimated.insert(format!("read-through retirement/recapture {label} ({rows} stored rows; deletion subset depends on activation time)"));
   157	                    } else {
   158	                        estimate.deadline_rewrite_rows = estimate.deadline_rewrite_rows.saturating_add(rows);
   159	                        estimate.unestimated.insert(format!("read-through {label} rewrites {rows} stored rows to recompute deadlines"));
   160	                    }
   161	                }
   162	            }
   163	            let plan = &plans[name];
   164	            let paths = table
   165	                .recorded
   166	                .keys()
   167	                .filter(|path| {
   168	                    previous
   169	                        .table(name)
   170	                        .is_none_or(|old| !old.recorded.contains_key(*path))
     1	//! Only post-program bookkeeping is buffered. Row/index/maintain writes still
     2	//! execute immediately, so every program-time read sees earlier writes.
     3	//! @ref LLP 3000.009#4.2 — reuse the reserved head and batch the commit tail.
     4	use super::store::{Tx, signed};
     5	use crate::{RuntimeError, Value, value::canonical_json};
     6	
     7	#[derive(Default)]
     8	pub(super) struct Tail {
     9	    pub commit: Option<Commit>,
    10	    pub partitions: Vec<String>,
    11	    pub ords: Vec<i64>,
    12	    pub tables: Vec<String>,
    13	    pub kinds: Vec<String>,
    14	    pub ids: Vec<String>,
    15	    pub data: Vec<Option<String>>,
    16	    pub effects: Vec<(String, String, Value)>,
    17	}
    18	pub(super) struct Commit {
    19	    pub seq: u64, pub at: i64, pub program: String, pub principal: String,
    20	    pub kind: String, pub touched: String, pub writes: u64,
    21	}
    22	
    23	impl Tx<'_> {
    24	    pub(super) fn flush_tail(&self) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    25	        let tail = std::mem::take(&mut *self.tail.borrow_mut());
    26	        let Some(commit) = tail.commit else { return Ok(()) };
    27	        let ids: Vec<_> = tail.effects.iter().map(|e| e.0.as_str()).collect();
    28	        let effects: Vec<_> = tail.effects.iter().map(|e| e.1.as_str()).collect();
    29	        let payloads: Vec<_> = tail.effects.iter().map(|e| canonical_json(&e.2)).collect();
    30	        // Synchronous postgres cannot pipeline independently prepared calls.
    31	        // One data-modifying CTE sends head, commit, all events and effects in
    32	        // one execution. The protected receipt writer remains the next call.
    33	        let rows = self.query("WITH head AS (
    34	            UPDATE _head SET seq = $1::bigint WHERE singleton AND seq = $1::bigint - 1 RETURNING seq
    35	        ), committed AS (
    36	            INSERT INTO _commits SELECT seq,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7 FROM head RETURNING seq
    37	        ), logged AS (
    38	            INSERT INTO _log SELECT l.partition,c.seq,l.ord,l.t,l.kind,l.id,l.data FROM committed c
    39	            CROSS JOIN unnest($8::text[],$9::bigint[],$10::text[],$11::text[],$12::text[],$13::text[]) AS l(partition,ord,t,kind,id,data)
    40	        ), effects AS (
    41	            INSERT INTO _outbox SELECT e.id,c.seq,e.effect,e.payload,'queued',0,$2 FROM committed c
    42	            CROSS JOIN unnest($14::text[],$15::text[],$16::text[]) AS e(id,effect,payload)
    43	        ) SELECT count(*) FROM committed", &[
    44	            &signed(commit.seq)?, &commit.at, &commit.program, &commit.principal, &commit.kind, &commit.touched, &signed(commit.writes)?,
    45	            &tail.partitions, &tail.ords, &tail.tables, &tail.kinds, &tail.ids, &tail.data, &ids, &effects, &payloads,
    46	        ])?;
    47	        if rows[0].get::<_, i64>(0) != 1 {
    48	            return Err(RuntimeError::Store("Postgres commit head changed without a serialization failure".into()));
    49	        }
    50	        Ok(())
    51	    }
    52	}

diff --git a/snapback4/lean/README.md b/snapback4/lean/README.md
index 3874b43a4f..28adb8d6cc 100644
--- a/snapback4/lean/README.md
+++ b/snapback4/lean/README.md
@@ -50,6 +50,7 @@ elan.
 | Values | `Quarry/Value.lean` | Scalars: null, bool, i53 int, text (as code points), nominal id, bytes. Values: scalars, arrays, rows. The one total order. A self-delimiting order-preserving key encoding. |
 | Syntax | `Quarry/Syntax.lean` | Schema: tables, typed nullable columns, unique keys with `else CODE`, read/insert/update/delete rules. Rules: allow, deny, `=`, `!=`, `exists t[..]` probes, and, or, not, over the row, old/next images and `viewer`. Expressions: arithmetic, comparison, and/or/not, `!` / `else`, `??`, conditionals, pure helper calls, arrays, records, point reads, pages, `count`, `exists`. Statements: `x = e`, `require`, `if`, `return`, and insert/upsert/update/delete. |
 | Store and rules | `Quarry/Store.lean` | Rows, the store, rule evaluation, visibility (`visibleRows`), a rule's disjuncts and terms. |
+| Maintained identity | `Quarry/Maintained.lean` | An independent logical abstraction of the versioned aggregate digest: every logical group, including the empty tuple, has a 64-character identity within the id bound; adoption replaces legacy ids and publishes the version in one state. It does not reproduce or verify SHA-256. |
 | Evaluator | `Quarry/Eval.lean` | `evalExpr` and `execStmts`: total and structural on fuel. The page walk, `complete` and `next`, counts and search `exists`; when a scan is decided (its pins, or a page guard: a grant or a pinned parent probe). Reads see only a `View` of visible rows. |
 | Writes | `Quarry/Write.lean` | Insert, upsert, update and delete, with rule checks and the documented refusal codes (`E_RULE`, `NOT_FOUND`, `E_CONFLICT`, `E_CONSTRAINT`, `E_REFERENCED`, application codes). A delete applies its references' `on delete` actions (LLP 3000.039 §3): the read prerequisite, the cascade closure, restrict probes outside it, set null, removal. Caps are carried as unenforced declarations: the emitter admits a capped table only when the program never inserts into or updates it. |
 | Calendar | `Quarry/Calendar.lean` | The UTC builtins of LLP 3000.040 §2.2: `day`, `hour`, `bucket` as period ordinals; `month`, `year`, `monthStart`, `yearStart` over the proleptic Gregorian calendar, by a 400/100/4/1-year decomposition independent of the engine's algorithm. Unbounded `Int`; the evaluator refuses `E_OVERFLOW` outside i53. |

+#[test]
+fn ungrouped_view_is_a_scalar_with_one_hidden_physical_group() {
+    let bound = compile(r#"view total: count posts
+  read <- allow
+  online only
+query totalPosts():
+  return total
+"#);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    assert_eq!(bound.programs[0].result, snapback4_lang::types::Ty::Int);
+    let maintain = &bound.schema.maintains[0];
+    assert!(maintain.by.is_empty());
+    let target = &bound.schema.tables["total"];
+    assert_eq!(target.columns.get("$global"), Some(&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Bool));
+    assert_eq!(target.indexes["byGroup"].columns(), ["$global"]);
+    let encoded = serde_json::to_value(&bound.programs[0].program).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(encoded["body"][0]["return"]["get"]["index"], "byGroup");
+    assert_eq!(encoded["body"][0]["return"]["get"]["key"], serde_json::json!([true]));
+}

- [write_references.rs:105–108](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-w12-lang/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/write_references.rs:105): The new preflight treats read-through children **already inside the cascade closure** as blockers. An admitted chain—expiring root → ordinary cascade child → non-expiring read-through cascade grandchild—therefore raises `E_PARENT_HAS_CHILDREN`, although deletion order would remove the grandchild first. Scheduled sweeps permanently defer this root; born-expired retirement refuses the transaction. Preflight must distinguish physical descendants outside the closure from descendants scheduled for deletion.

- [write_references.rs:105–108](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-w12-lang/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/write_references.rs:105): The new preflight treats read-through children **already inside the cascade closure** as blockers. An admitted chain—expiring root → ordinary cascade child → non-expiring read-through cascade grandchild—therefore raises `E_PARENT_HAS_CHILDREN`, although deletion order would remove the grandchild first. Scheduled sweeps permanently defer this root; born-expired retirement refuses the transaction. Preflight must distinguish physical descendants outside the closure from descendants scheduled for deletion.
